0004c0c0     SDL_TEXCOORD_PRECISION vec2 uv_texels = uv * u_texel_size.zw + 0.5;
0004c109     // get uvs for the center of the 4 surrounding texels by flooring
0004c14f     SDL_TEXCOORD_PRECISION vec4 uv_min_max = vec4((floor(uv_texels) - 0.5) * u_texel_size.xy, (floor(uv_texels) + 0.5) * u_texel_size.xy);
0004c1db     // blend factor
0004c1ef     SDL_TEXCOORD_PRECISION vec2 uv_frac = fract(uv_texels);
0004c22c     // sample all 4 texels
0004c247     mediump vec4 texelA = SamplePaletteNearest(uv_min_max.xy);
0004c286     mediump vec4 texelB = SamplePaletteNearest(uv_min_max.xw);
0004c2c5     mediump vec4 texelC = SamplePaletteNearest(uv_min_max.zy);
0004c304     mediump vec4 texelD = SamplePaletteNearest(uv_min_max.zw);
0004c344     // bilinear interpolation
0004c362     return mix(mix(texelA, texelB, uv_frac.y), mix(texelC, texelD, uv_frac.y), uv_frac.x);

0004eb7c void main()

0004eba0     lowp vec3 rgb;
0004ebb4     // Get the YUV values 
0004ebcf     yuv.x = texture2D(u_texture,   v_texCoord).r;
0004ec01     yuv.y = texture2D(u_texture_u, v_texCoord).r;
0004ec33     yuv.z = texture2D(u_texture_v, v_texCoord).r;
0004ec66     // Do the color transform 
0004ec85     yuv += u_offset;
0004ec9a     rgb = yuv * u_matrix;
0004ecb5     // That was easy. :) 
0004eccf     gl_FragColor = vec4(rgb, 1);
0004ecf0     gl_FragColor *= v_color;

0004eff1 varying SDL_TEXCOORD_PRECISION vec2 v_texCoord;
0004f022 void main()
0004f030     mediump vec3 yuv;
0004f046     lowp vec3 rgb;
0004f05a     // Get the YUV values 
0004f075     yuv.x = texture2D(u_texture,   v_texCoord).r;
0004f0a7     yuv.yz = texture2D(u_texture_u, v_texCoord).rg;
0004f0dc     // Do the color transform 
0004f0fb     yuv += u_offset;
0004f110     rgb = yuv * u_matrix;
0004f12b     // That was easy. :) 
0004f145     gl_FragColor = vec4(rgb, 1);
0004f166     gl_FragColor *= v_color;
